**gRPC metrics modernization**: Migrate from deprecated `go-grpc-prometheus` to `go-grpc-middleware/providers/prometheus` for gRPC metrics instrumentation (#5272)\\n43: \\n44: ### Dependencies\\n45: \\n46: - Updated various dependencies including OpenTelemetry, OPA, gRPC ecosystem, ClickHouse client, and UI libraries\\n47: \\n48: ## [2.5.0](https://github.com/flipt-io/flipt/releases/tag/v2.5.0) - 2026-01-09\\n49: \\n50: ### Added\\n51: \\n52: - **Custom commit and PR templates**: Configure server-wide default templates for commit messages, PR titles, and PR bodies per storage backend (#5091)\\n53: - **GitHub auth fallbacks**: Provide sensible fallback values for name and email when GitHub users don't have public profile information configured (#5154)\\n54: - **SSH remote URL normalization**: Automatically normalize HTTPS URLs to SCP-style format when SSH credentials are provided, ensuring Git operations work correctly (#5130)\\n55: \\n56: ### Fixed\\n57: \\n58: - **OIDC secret resolution**: Resolve secret references (`${secret:file:...}`) in OIDC provider configuration for `client_id` and `client_secret` fields (#5153)\\n59: \\n60: ### Dependencies\\n61: \\n62: - **Git connection error handling**: Properly detect and handle DNS resolution errors and I/O timeouts when checking git repository connections, allowing lenient fetch policy to work correctly when remotes are temporarily unreachable (#4965)\\n74: - 2025-08-12\\n259: \\n260: Flipt v2 is a complete re-architecture of Flipt, focused on Git-native operations and multi-environment support. This major release represents a fundamental shift in how Flipt manages feature flags, bringing enterprise-grade GitOps workflows to feature management.\\n261: \\n262: ### Added\\n263: \\n264: #### Core Features\\n265: \\n266: - **Git-Native Architecture**: Feature flags are now stored directly in Git repositories with full read/write capabilities through the API and UI\\n267: - **Multi-Environment Support**: Manage feature flags across multiple environments using:\\n268:   - Different Git repositories per environment\\n269:   - Different directories within the same repository\\n270:   - Different branches within the same repository\\n271: - **Environment Branching**: Create isolated branches of any environment for testing changes without affecting production\\n272: - **Git SCM Integration** (Pro): Native integration with GitHub, GitLab, BitBucket, Azure DevOps, and Gitea for merge proposals and GPG-signed commits\\n273: - **Offline Mode**: Continue serving flags even when the source Git repository is temporarily unavailable\\n274: \\n275: #### Security & Secrets Management\\n276: \\n277: - **Secret References in Configuration**: Support for referencing secrets from environment variables and files using `{{ secret \\\"key\\\" }}` syntax\\n278: - **Integrated Secrets Management**: Built-in secure storage for GPG keys with HashiCorp Vault integration (Pro) and file system support (OSS)\\n279: - **GPG Commit Signing** (Pro): Sign all Git commits for maximum security and auditability\\n280: - **JWT Claims Mapping**: Configurable mapping of JWT claims to well-known authentication metadata\\n281: \\n282: #### UI & Developer Experience\\n283: \\n284: - **Redesigned UI**: Complete UI overhaul for improved user experience and intuitive navigation\\n285: - **Environment Switcher**: Seamless switching between environments in the UI\\n286: - **Merge Proposals**: Create pull/merge requests directly from the UI (Pro)\\n287: \\n288: ### Changed\\n289: \\n290: - **Storage Backend**: Default storage is now Git-based (memory or disk) rather than database-backed\\n291: - **Configuration Format**: New configuration structure to support environments and Git storage options\\n292: - **API Versioning**: New v2 APIs for environments, analytics, and evaluation while maintaining v1 compatibility\\n293: - **License Model**: Introduction of Pro tier with enterprise features while maintaining OSS core\\n294: \",\"url\":\"https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/flipt-io/flipt@2a8dc05e0bd0771709e439358b969e2ef9964345/-/blob/CHANGELOG.md\"}","structuredContent":{"content":"1: # Changelog\n2: \n3: This format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/)\n4: and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).\n5: \n6: ## [2.7.0](https://github.com/flipt-io/flipt/releases/tag/v2.7.0) - 2025-08-12\n259: \n260: Flipt v2 is a complete re-architecture of Flipt, focused on Git-native operations and multi-environment support. This major release represents a fundamental shift in how Flipt manages feature flags, bringing enterprise-grade GitOps workflows to feature management.\n261: \n262: ### Added\n263: \n264: #### Core Features\n265: \n266: - **Git-Native Architecture**: Feature flags are now stored directly in Git repositories with full read/write capabilities through the API and UI\n267: - **Multi-Environment Support**: Manage feature flags across multiple environments using:\n268:   - Different Git repositories per environment\n269:   - Different directories within the same repository\n270:   - Different branches within the same repository\n271: - **Environment Branching**: Create isolated branches of any environment for testing changes without affecting production\n272: - **Git SCM Integration** (Pro): Native integration with GitHub, GitLab, BitBucket, Azure DevOps, and Gitea for merge proposals and GPG-signed commits\n273: - **Offline Mode**: Continue serving flags even when the source Git repository is temporarily unavailable\n274: \n275: #### Security & Secrets Management\n276: \n277: - **Secret References in Configuration**: Support for referencing secrets from environment variables and files using `{{ secret \"key\" }}` syntax\n278: - **Integrated Secrets Management**: Built-in secure storage for GPG keys with HashiCorp Vault integration (Pro) and file system support (OSS)\n279: - **GPG Commit Signing** (Pro): Sign all Git commits for maximum security and auditability\n280: - **JWT Claims Mapping**: Configurable mapping of JWT claims to well-known authentication metadata\n281: \n282: #### UI & Developer Experience\n283: \n284: - **Redesigned UI**: Complete UI overhaul for improved user experience and intuitive navigation\n285: - **Environment Switcher**: Seamless switching between environments in the UI\n286: - **Merge Proposals**: Create pull/merge requests directly from the UI (Pro)\n287: \n288: ### Changed\n289: \n290: - **Storage Backend**: Default storage is now Git-based (memory or disk) rather than database-backed\n291: - **Configuration Format**: New configuration structure to support environments and Git storage options\n292: - {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_015NzFRPWhv5RWKZ8vEoYLmk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Po5Z58kq9vGRfRRjgY4VAS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/RELEASE_NOTES.md","content":"# Flipt Release Notes\n\nComprehensive summary of API changes in recent Flipt releases. Flipt is a Git-native feature flag management system with REST and gRPC APIs for managing feature flags, segments, and environments.\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n- [Breaking Changes](#breaking-changes)\n- [New Features](#new-features)\n- [Deprecations](#deprecations)\n- [Bug Fixes](#bug-fixes)\n- [Upgrade Guide](#upgrade-guide)\n\n---\n\n## Breaking Changes\n\nThese changes require action from users upgrading.\n\n### Configuration Schema Changes (v2.0+)\n\n**Affected API:** Storage Configuration\n**Since:** v2.0.0\n\nThe storage configuration format has fundamentally changed to support Git-native operations:\n\n**Before (v1.x):**\n```yaml\n# Database-backed storage\nstorage:\n  type: postgres\n  connection_string: \"...\"\n```\n\n**After (v2.0+):**\n```yaml\n# Git-based storage\nstorage:\n  default:\n    remote: \"https://github.com/user/flags.git\"\n    branch: \"main\"\n    backend:\n      type: \"memory\"  # or \"local\"\n    credentials:\n      type: \"github\"\n      token: \"...\"\n```\n\n**Migration Path:**\n1. Migrating from v1 Flipt requires exporting all flags and segments from the old database\n2. Create a new Git repository for storing feature flag definitions\n3. Initialize Flipt with Git storage configuration pointing to the new repository\n4. Import previously exported flags via the v2 API\n5. Refer to [Git Storage Configuration Documentation](https://docs.flipt.io/latest/configuration/storage) for detailed steps\n\n### REST API v1 Endpoint Changes\n\n**Affected API:** `/api/v1/*` endpoints\n**Status:** Deprecated (still available but not recommended)\n\nWhile v1 endpoints are preserved for backwards compatibility, they are no longer the primary API surface. Migrate to v2 APIs:\n\n**v1 ListFlags Example:**\n```\nGET /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags\n```\n\n**v2 Equivalent:**\n```\nGET /api/v2/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/resources/flipt.core.Flag\n```\n\n**Migration Guide:**\n- Switch from `/api/v1/` to `/api/v2/environments/{environment_key}/` endpoints\n- Use `google.protobuf.Any` payload in v2 resources API for flag and segment data\n- Include `revision` field in update/delete requests for optimistic concurrency control\n\n### Evaluation Environment Key Parameter (v2.3.0+)\n\n**Affected API:** Evaluation Service\n**Since:** v2.3.0\n\nThe `environment_key` field is now **required** in all evaluation requests (previously optional with fallback to default environment):\n\n**Before:**\n```json\n{\n  \"request_id\": \"req-123\",\n  \"namespace_key\": \"default\",\n  \"flag_key\": \"my_flag\",\n  \"entity_id\": \"user-456\",\n  \"context\": {}\n}\n```\n\n**After:**\n```json\n{\n  \"request_id\": \"req-123\",\n  \"environment_key\": \"production\",\n  \"namespace_key\": \"default\",\n  \"flag_key\": \"my_flag\",\n  \"entity_id\": \"user-456\",\n  \"context\": {}\n}\n```\n\n**Migration Path:**\n1. Update all evaluation client SDK calls to explicitly provide the `environment_key`\n2. SDKs should no longer rely on automatic fallback to the default environment\n3. Ensure environment context is available before making evaluation requests\n\n### gRPC Metrics Instrumentation Change (v2.6.0)\n\n**Affected API:** gRPC metrics collection\n**Since:** v2.6.0\n\nMigration from deprecated `go-grpc-prometheus` to `go-grpc-middleware/providers/prometheus`:\n\n**Impact:**\n- gRPC metric names and labels may differ slightly\n- Prometheus scrape configurations should remain compatible\n- Update any metric-based dashboards or alerts that rely on specific metric names\n\n**Migration Path:**\n1. Update Prometheus scrape configurations if needed\n2. Review metric names in monitoring/alerting rules\n3. Refer to [gRPC Metrics Documentation](https://grpc-ecosystem.github.io/grpc-middleware/) for new metric specifications\n\n### Authentication Error Handling (v2.4.0)\n\n**Affected API:** Authentication Service\n**Since:** v2.4.0\n\nAuthentication errors are now properly returned instead of panicking. If your code relied on panic recovery, update error handling:\n\n**Before:**\n```go\n// Panic could occur in EmailMatchingUnaryInterceptor\n```\n\n**After:**\n```go\n// Proper error returned in context\nfunc (mi *EmailMatchingUnaryInterceptor) skipped(ctx context.Context, server *grpc.Server) bool {\n    // Returns error instead of panicking\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## New Features\n\n### SigV4 Authentication for Analytics (v2.7.0)\n\n**API:** Analytics Prometheus Storage Backend\n**Endpoints Affected:**\n- `/internal/v2/analytics/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags/{flag_key}`\n- `/internal/v2/analytics/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/batch`\n\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nanalytics:\n  storage:\n    prometheus:\n      auth:\n        type: \"sigv4\"\n        region: \"us-east-1\"\n        service: \"aps\"  # Amazon Prometheus Service\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Enables direct integration with Amazon Managed Service for Prometheus (AMP) with automatic request signing.\n\n### GitHub App Authentication for Git SCM (v2.6.0)\n\n**API:** Git Storage Credentials\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nstorage:\n  default:\n    credentials:\n      type: \"github_app\"\n      app_id: \"123456\"\n      installation_id: \"789012\"\n      private_key: \"${secret:file:path/to/key}\"\n```\n\n**Benefits:**\n- More granular permissions than personal access tokens\n- Better for automated deployments\n- Eliminates PAT rotation requirements\n- Supported in v2 environments API\n\n**Migration from PAT:**\n```yaml\n# Before\ncredentials:\n  type: \"github\"\n  token: \"ghp_xxx\"\n\n# After\ncredentials:\n  type: \"github_app\"\n  app_id: \"...\"\n  installation_id: \"...\"\n  private_key: \"...\"\n```\n\n### OIDC Enhancements (v2.6.0, v2.5.0)\n\n#### UserInfo Claims Merging\n```yaml\nauthentication:\n  methods:\n    oidc:\n      - name: okta\n        issuer_url: \"https://dev.okta.com\"\n        client_id: \"...\"\n        client_secret: \"...\"\n        scopes: [\"openid\", \"profile\", \"email\"]\n        fetch_user_info: true  # NEW: fetch and merge UserInfo claims\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Providers that keep minimal claims in ID tokens can now fetch additional claims from UserInfo endpoint.\n\n#### Configurable Signing Algorithms\n```yaml\nauthentication:\n  methods:\n    oidc:\n      - name: custom-provider\n        issuer_url: \"https://custom.example.com\"\n        algorithms: [\"RS256\", \"ES256\", \"HS256\"]  # NEW: support multiple algorithms\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Support OIDC providers that don't use RS256 for ID token signing.\n\n#### Secret References in OIDC Config (v2.5.0)\n```yaml\nauthentication:\n  methods:\n    oidc:\n      - name: okta\n        client_id: \"${secret:file:okta/client_id}\"\n        client_secret: \"${secret:file:okta/client_secret}\"\n```\n\n**Syntax:** `${secret:file:path/to/secret}` or `${secret:env:ENV_VAR_NAME}`\n\n### Configurable Flag Metadata in ListFlags (v2.3.0)\n\n**API:** REST and gRPC ListFlags endpoints\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nserver:\n  api:\n    list_flags:\n      include_metadata: false  # Default: only include when explicitly requested\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Control whether flag metadata is exposed in public ListFlags responses. Useful for hiding internal metadata in public APIs.\n\n**Example Request:**\n```\nGET /api/v1/namespaces/default/flags?include_metadata=true\n```\n\n### Git Fetch Policy Configuration (v2.3.0)\n\n**API:** Storage Backend Configuration\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nstorage:\n  default:\n    fetch_policy: \"lenient\"  # or \"strict\" (default)\n```\n\n**Options:**\n- `strict` (default): Fail startup if remote fetch fails\n- `lenient`: Continue with stale local data if fetch fails\n\n**Use Case:** Enable offline/degraded mode for resilience when Git remote is temporarily unavailable.\n\n### Custom Commit and PR Templates (v2.5.0)\n\n**API:** Storage Configuration\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nstorage:\n  default:\n    templates:\n      commit_message: \"[flipt] {{.Resource}} {{.Action}}\\n\\nAutomated change by {{.Author}}\"\n      proposal_title: \"Flipt: Update features on {{.Base.Ref}}\"\n      proposal_body: \"Automated proposal\\n\\n- Branch: {{.Branch.Ref}}\\n- Base: {{.Base.Ref}}\"\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Customize commit messages and pull request titles/bodies for Git integration.\n\n### Boolean Evaluation Enhanced (v2.2.0)\n\n**API:** Evaluation Service\n**New Response Field:** Boolean evaluation responses now include matched `segment_keys`\n\n**Example:**\n```json\n{\n  \"enabled\": true,\n  \"reason\": \"MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON\",\n  \"segment_keys\": [\"segment_1\", \"segment_2\"],\n  \"flag_key\": \"my_flag\",\n  \"timestamp\": \"2026-02-17T00:00:00Z\"\n}\n```\n\n**Benefit:** Provides visibility into which segments matched, improving debuggability.\n\n### Streaming Evaluation (v2.1.1+)\n\n**API:** ClientEvaluationService\n**Endpoint:**\n```\nGET /client/v2/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{key}/stream\n```\n\n**Message Types:**\n```protobuf\nrpc EvaluationSnapshotNamespaceStream(EvaluationNamespaceSnapshotStreamRequest)\n  returns (stream EvaluationNamespaceSnapshot)\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Real-time evaluation data streaming for dashboard updates or client-side caching.\n\n---\n\n## Deprecations\n\n### v1 API Endpoints (Deprecated since v2.0.0)\n\n**Endpoints Affected:**\n- `GET /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags`\n- `GET /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags/{flag_key}`\n- `POST /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags`\n- And other `/api/v1/*` endpoints\n\n**Replacement:** Use v2 Environments and Resources APIs:\n```\nGET /api/v2/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/resources/flipt.core.Flag\n```\n\n**Timeline:** Removal planned ~1 year after v2.0.0 (August 2025 → August 2026)\n\n### Database-Backed Storage (Deprecated since v2.0.0)\n\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\n# DEPRECATED - do not use\nstorage:\n  type: postgres\n  connection_string: \"...\"\n```\n\n**Replacement:** Git-based storage is now the standard\n```yaml\n# Use this instead\nstorage:\n  default:\n    remote: \"...\"\n    backend:\n      type: \"memory\"\n```\n\n**Timeline:** Complete removal planned ~1 year after v2.0.0\n\n### Session Token Offset Parameter (Deprecated in v2.0+)\n\n**API:** ListFlagRequest\n**Deprecated Field:**\n```protobuf\nmessage ListFlagRequest {\n  int32 limit = 1;\n  int32 offset = 2 [deprecated = true];  // DEPRECATED: use page_token instead\n  string page_token = 3;  // USE THIS\n  ...\n}\n```\n\n**Migration:**\n```go\n// Before\nGetFlags(ctx, &flipt.ListFlagRequest{\n  Offset: 0,\n  Limit: 10,\n})\n\n// After\nGetFlags(ctx, &flipt.ListFlagRequest{\n  Limit: 10,\n  PageToken: \"\", // empty string for first page\n})\n```\n\n### OpenTelemetry gRPC Interceptors (Deprecated in v2.4.0)\n\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\n# DEPRECATED gRPC interceptor style\nserver:\n  grpc:\n    interceptors:\n      - otel\n```\n\n**Replacement:** Stats handler approach (automatically used in v2.4.0+)\n```go\n// Modern approach - automatically configured\n// Use stats handler for complete tracing coverage\n```\n\n---\n\n## Bug Fixes\n\n### Critical Fixes\n\n#### Segment Deletion Validation (v2.3.0)\n- **Issue:** Segments could be deleted even when referenced by rules or rollouts\n- **Fix:** Added validation to prevent deletion of segments with active references\n- **Impact:** Database integrity protection\n\n#### Streaming Evaluation HTTP/2 Support (v2.1.1)\n- **Issue:** Streaming evaluation only worked over gRPC, not HTTP/2\n- **Fix:** Accept HTTP/2 connections for streaming evaluation endpoints\n- **Impact:** Broader client compatibility\n\n#### Environment Context Handling (v2.1.1)\n- **Issue:** Error not returned when environment not found in context\n- **Fix:** Properly return error from `GetFromContext` when environment missing\n- **Impact:** Better error diagnostics\n\n### Important Fixes\n\n#### Git Remote Synchronization (v2.4.0)\n- **Issue:** Git remote URLs could become stale if configuration changed\n- **Fix:** Auto-sync remote URLs when configuration is updated\n- **Impact:** Prevents sync failures after config changes\n\n#### Git Connection Error Handling (v2.4.0)\n- **Issue:** DNS resolution and I/O timeout errors not properly detected\n- **Fix:** Properly handle connection errors to allow lenient fetch policy to work\n- **Impact:** Better offline mode support\n\n#### Git Ref Update After Fetch (v2.6.0)\n- **Issue:** Shallow fetches didn't persist references to `refs/heads/`\n- **Fix:** Complete ref update for both bare and normal repositories\n- **Impact:** Proper branch tracking in Git operations\n\n#### Evaluation Environment Lookup (v2.3.1)\n- **Issue:** Duplicate environment retrieval logic with inconsistent fallback behavior\n- **Fix:** Extracted reusable helper method for consistent lookup\n- **Impact:** Reduced code duplication and bugs\n\n#### License Key Format Handling (v2.6.0)\n- **Issue:** License activation wizard failed with annual license key format\n- **Fix:** Support both monthly and annual license key formats\n- **Impact:** Improved Pro license activation UX\n\n#### Redis Configuration Schema (v2.3.0)\n- **Issue:** Redis mode configuration accepted empty string\n- **Fix:** Set default Redis mode to \"single\" instead of empty string\n- **Impact:** More explicit configuration validation\n\n#### Form Submission UX (v2.3.0, v2.3.1)\n- **Issue:** Enter key in tag input submitted entire form unexpectedly\n- **Fix:** Properly handle Enter key for tag addition vs form submission\n- **Impact:** Better UI form handling\n\n### Analytics Fixes\n\n#### Batch Analytics Processing (v2.1.3)\n- **Issue:** Batch analytics returned partial data for requested flags\n- **Fix:** Return all batch data for requested flags\n- **Impact:** Complete analytics visibility\n\n#### Analytics Metadata Events (v2.3.0)\n- **Issue:** Batch evaluation endpoint missing OpenTelemetry tracking\n- **Fix:** Add event tracking to batch evaluation for observability\n- **Impact:** Complete tracing of batch operations\n\n---\n\n## Upgrade Guide\n\n### General Upgrade Process\n\n1. **Backup Configuration and Data**\n   ```bash\n   # Export current flags if upgrading from v1\n   flipt export --format=json > flags_backup.json\n\n   # Backup Git repository\n   git clone --mirror https://your-repo.git flipt-backup.git.mirror\n   ```\n\n2. **Update Flipt Binary**\n   ```bash\n   # Using official releases\n   curl -fsSL https://install.flipt.io | bash\n\n   # Or using container\n   docker pull flipt/flipt:v2.7.0\n   ```\n\n3. **Update Configuration File**\n   Review `/etc/flipt/config.yml` (or your config location) for required changes:\n   - Storage configuration format\n   - Authentication method configurations\n   - Any deprecated options\n\n4. **Verify Environment Variables**\n   Check all `FLIPT_*` environment variables are compatible with new version\n\n5. **Start Flipt**\n   ```bash\n   systemctl restart flipt\n   # or\n   docker-compose up -d\n   ```\n\n6. **Verify Endpoints**\n   Test core endpoints:\n   ```bash\n   # Health check\n   curl http://localhost:8080/health\n\n   # Verify APIs\n   curl -H \"Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN\" http://localhost:8080/api/v2/environments\n   ```\n\n### Upgrading from v1.x to v2.0+\n\nThis is a major upgrade requiring data migration:\n\n**Step 1: Set Up Git Repository**\n```bash\n# Create new Git repository for flags\nmkdir flipt-features && cd flipt-features\ngit init\ngit branch -m main\n\n# Or use existing repository\ngit clone https://github.com/your-org/flipt-features.git\ncd flipt-features\n```\n\n**Step 2: Update Configuration**\n```yaml\n# New v2 configuration\nstorage:\n  default:\n    remote: \"https://github.com/your-org/flipt-features.git\"\n    branch: \"main\"\n    backend:\n      type: \"local\"\n      path: \"/var/lib/flipt/storage\"\n    credentials:\n      type: \"github\"\n      token: \"${secret:file:/etc/flipt/github_token}\"\n\nenvironments:\n  default:\n    storage: \"default\"\n```\n\n**Step 3: Migrate Flags (if applicable)**\n```bash\n# Option A: Export from v1, import to v2\nflipt export --format=json > /tmp/flags.json\nflipt import --format=json < /tmp/flags.json\n\n# Option B: Manually recreate flags via v2 API\n# See Section \"New Features\" for v2 API examples\n```\n\n**Step 4: Update Client SDKs**\n```go\n// Before (v1)\nclient := flipt.NewClient(\n    flipt.WithAddress(\"localhost:8080\"),\n)\nresult, _ := client.Evaluation.Boolean(ctx, &flipt.EvaluationRequest{\n    NamespaceKey: \"default\",\n    FlagKey: \"my_flag\",\n    EntityId: \"user-123\",\n    Context: map[string]string{},\n})\n\n// After (v2)\nclient := flipt.NewClient(\n    flipt.WithAddress(\"localhost:8080\"),\n)\nresult, _ := client.Evaluation.Boolean(ctx, &flipt.EvaluationRequest{\n    EnvironmentKey: \"production\",  // NEW: required\n    NamespaceKey: \"default\",\n    FlagKey: \"my_flag\",\n    EntityId: \"user-123\",\n    Context: map[string]string{},\n})\n```\n\n### Upgrading from v2.x to v2.y\n\nThese are minor/patch upgrades with good backwards compatibility:\n\n**For v2.5.0 → v2.6.0:**\n- GitHub App auth is now available as an alternative to PAT\n- OIDC configuration enhancements are optional\n- No breaking changes; upgrade and redeploy\n\n**For v2.6.0 → v2.7.0:**\n- Go 1.26 and go-git v6 dependencies updated\n- SigV4 auth for Prometheus is optional\n- Default Git commit email updated (more appropriate value)\n- No breaking changes; standard upgrade\n\n**For any v2.x upgrade:**\n```bash\n# 1. Pull latest version\ndocker pull flipt/flipt:v2.7.0\n\n# 2. Verify configuration compatibility\nflipt validate-config --config=/etc/flipt/config.yml\n\n# 3. Restart Flipt\nsystemctl restart flipt\n# or\ndocker-compose up -d --force-recreate\n\n# 4. Check logs for warnings/errors\njournalctl -u flipt -f\n# or\ndocker-compose logs -f flipt\n```\n\n### Configuration Validation\n\nFlipt provides a validation command:\n```bash\n# Validate configuration before deploying\nflipt validate-config --config=./config.yml\n\n# With verbose output\nflipt validate-config --config=./config.yml --verbose\n```\n\n### Monitoring the Upgrade\n\nKey metrics to monitor:\n\n1. **API Error Rate**\n   ```promql\n   rate(flipt_server_request_duration_count{status=\"error\"}[5m])\n   ```\n\n2. **Evaluation Latency**\n   ```promql\n   histogram_quantile(0.95,\n     rate(flipt_evaluation_duration_seconds_bucket[5m])\n   )\n   ```\n\n3. **Git Sync Status**\n   ```promql\n   flipt_git_sync_duration_seconds_bucket\n   flipt_git_sync_errors_total\n   ```\n\n### Rollback Procedures\n\n**For Docker Deployments:**\n```bash\n# Quickly rollback to previous version\ndocker-compose down\ndocker-compose.yml  # Restore previous version\ndocker-compose up -d\n```\n\n**For Systemd Deployments:**\n```bash\nsystemctl stop flipt\n# Restore previous binary version\n/usr/local/bin/flipt  # downgrade\nsystemctl start flipt\n```\n\n**For Kubernetes Deployments:**\n```bash\n# Rollback to previous deployment\nkubectl rollout undo deployment/flipt\nkubectl rollout status deployment/flipt\n```\n\n### Common Troubleshooting\n\n**Issue: 404 errors on v1 API endpoints**\n- **Cause:** v1 endpoints are still available but require explicit migration\n- **Solution:** Update client SDKs to use v2 APIs as documented in \"Breaking Changes\"\n\n**Issue: Git authentication failures after upgrade**\n- **Cause:** Credentials format may have changed\n- **Solution:** Verify Git credentials in configuration using secret references\n\n**Issue: Evaluation requests return \"environment not found\"**\n- **Cause:** `environment_key` parameter now required (v2.3.0+)\n- **Solution:** Update evaluation requests to include explicit `environment_key` field\n\n**Issue: High memory usage after upgrade**\n- **Cause:** OpenTelemetry or ClickHouse integration in v2.1.3+ requires tuning\n- **Solution:** Review analytics storage configuration and batch buffer settings\n\n---\n\n## Summary Table\n\n| Feature | Introduced | Status | Migration Required |\n|---------|------------|--------|-------------------|\n| Git-native storage | v2.0.0 | Stable | Yes (from v1) |\n| Multi-environment support | v2.0.0 | Stable | Optional |\n| v2 Environments API | v2.0.0 | Stable | No |\n| Evaluation environment_key Flipt is a Git-native feature flag management system with REST and gRPC APIs for managing feature flags, segments, and environments.\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n- [Breaking Changes](#breaking-changes)\n- [New Features](#new-features)\n- [Deprecations](#deprecations)\n- [Bug Fixes](#bug-fixes)\n- [Upgrade Guide](#upgrade-guide)\n\n---\n\n## Breaking Changes\n\nThese changes require action from users upgrading.\n\n### Configuration Schema Changes (v2.0+)\n\n**Affected API:** Storage Configuration\n**Since:** v2.0.0\n\nThe storage configuration format has fundamentally changed to support Git-native operations:\n\n**Before (v1.x):**\n```yaml\n# Database-backed storage\nstorage:\n  type: postgres\n  connection_string: \"...\"\n```\n\n**After (v2.0+):**\n```yaml\n# Git-based storage\nstorage:\n  default:\n    remote: \"https://github.com/user/flags.git\"\n    branch: \"main\"\n    backend:\n      type: \"memory\"  # or \"local\"\n    credentials:\n      type: \"github\"\n      token: \"...\"\n```\n\n**Migration Path:**\n1. Migrating from v1 Flipt requires exporting all flags and segments from the old database\n2. Create a new Git repository for storing feature flag definitions\n3. Initialize Flipt with Git storage configuration pointing to the new repository\n4. Import previously exported flags via the v2 API\n5. Refer to [Git Storage Configuration Documentation](https://docs.flipt.io/latest/configuration/storage) for detailed steps\n\n### REST API v1 Endpoint Changes\n\n**Affected API:** `/api/v1/*` endpoints\n**Status:** Deprecated (still available but not recommended)\n\nWhile v1 endpoints are preserved for backwards compatibility, they are no longer the primary API surface. Migrate to v2 APIs:\n\n**v1 ListFlags Example:**\n```\nGET /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags\n```\n\n**v2 Equivalent:**\n```\nGET /api/v2/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/resources/flipt.core.Flag\n```\n\n**Migration Guide:**\n- Switch from `/api/v1/` to `/api/v2/environments/{environment_key}/` endpoints\n- Use `google.protobuf.Any` payload in v2 resources API for flag and segment data\n- Include `revision` field in update/delete requests for optimistic concurrency control\n\n### Evaluation Environment Key Parameter (v2.3.0+)\n\n**Affected API:** Evaluation Service\n**Since:** v2.3.0\n\nThe `environment_key` field is now **required** in all evaluation requests (previously optional with fallback to default environment):\n\n**Before:**\n```json\n{\n  \"request_id\": \"req-123\",\n  \"namespace_key\": \"default\",\n  \"flag_key\": \"my_flag\",\n  \"entity_id\": \"user-456\",\n  \"context\": {}\n}\n```\n\n**After:**\n```json\n{\n  \"request_id\": \"req-123\",\n  \"environment_key\": \"production\",\n  \"namespace_key\": \"default\",\n  \"flag_key\": \"my_flag\",\n  \"entity_id\": \"user-456\",\n  \"context\": {}\n}\n```\n\n**Migration Path:**\n1. Update all evaluation client SDK calls to explicitly provide the `environment_key`\n2. SDKs should no longer rely on automatic fallback to the default environment\n3. Ensure environment context is available before making evaluation requests\n\n### gRPC Metrics Instrumentation Change (v2.6.0)\n\n**Affected API:** gRPC metrics collection\n**Since:** v2.6.0\n\nMigration from deprecated `go-grpc-prometheus` to `go-grpc-middleware/providers/prometheus`:\n\n**Impact:**\n- gRPC metric names and labels may differ slightly\n- Prometheus scrape configurations should remain compatible\n- Update any metric-based dashboards or alerts that rely on specific metric names\n\n**Migration Path:**\n1. Update Prometheus scrape configurations if needed\n2. Review metric names in monitoring/alerting rules\n3. Refer to [gRPC Metrics Documentation](https://grpc-ecosystem.github.io/grpc-middleware/) for new metric specifications\n\n### Authentication Error Handling (v2.4.0)\n\n**Affected API:** Authentication Service\n**Since:** v2.4.0\n\nAuthentication errors are now properly returned instead of panicking. If your code relied on panic recovery, update error handling:\n\n**Before:**\n```go\n// Panic could occur in EmailMatchingUnaryInterceptor\n```\n\n**After:**\n```go\n// Proper error returned in context\nfunc (mi *EmailMatchingUnaryInterceptor) skipped(ctx context.Context, server *grpc.Server) bool {\n    // Returns error instead of panicking\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## New Features\n\n### SigV4 Authentication for Analytics (v2.7.0)\n\n**API:** Analytics Prometheus Storage Backend\n**Endpoints Affected:**\n- `/internal/v2/analytics/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags/{flag_key}`\n- `/internal/v2/analytics/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/batch`\n\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nanalytics:\n  storage:\n    prometheus:\n      auth:\n        type: \"sigv4\"\n        region: \"us-east-1\"\n        service: \"aps\"  # Amazon Prometheus Service\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Enables direct integration with Amazon Managed Service for Prometheus (AMP) with automatic request signing.\n\n### GitHub App Authentication for Git SCM (v2.6.0)\n\n**API:** Git Storage Credentials\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nstorage:\n  default:\n    credentials:\n      type: \"github_app\"\n      app_id: \"123456\"\n      installation_id: \"789012\"\n      private_key: \"${secret:file:path/to/key}\"\n```\n\n**Benefits:**\n- More granular permissions than personal access tokens\n- Better for automated deployments\n- Eliminates PAT rotation requirements\n- Supported in v2 environments API\n\n**Migration from PAT:**\n```yaml\n# Before\ncredentials:\n  type: \"github\"\n  token: \"ghp_xxx\"\n\n# After\ncredentials:\n  type: \"github_app\"\n  app_id: \"...\"\n  installation_id: \"...\"\n  private_key: \"...\"\n```\n\n### OIDC Enhancements (v2.6.0, v2.5.0)\n\n#### UserInfo Claims Merging\n```yaml\nauthentication:\n  methods:\n    oidc:\n      - name: okta\n        issuer_url: \"https://dev.okta.com\"\n        client_id: \"...\"\n        client_secret: \"...\"\n        scopes: [\"openid\", \"profile\", \"email\"]\n        fetch_user_info: true  # NEW: fetch and merge UserInfo claims\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Providers that keep minimal claims in ID tokens can now fetch additional claims from UserInfo endpoint.\n\n#### Configurable Signing Algorithms\n```yaml\nauthentication:\n  methods:\n    oidc:\n      - name: custom-provider\n        issuer_url: \"https://custom.example.com\"\n        algorithms: [\"RS256\", \"ES256\", \"HS256\"]  # NEW: support multiple algorithms\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Support OIDC providers that don't use RS256 for ID token signing.\n\n#### Secret References in OIDC Config (v2.5.0)\n```yaml\nauthentication:\n  methods:\n    oidc:\n      - name: okta\n        client_id: \"${secret:file:okta/client_id}\"\n        client_secret: \"${secret:file:okta/client_secret}\"\n```\n\n**Syntax:** `${secret:file:path/to/secret}` or `${secret:env:ENV_VAR_NAME}`\n\n### Configurable Flag Metadata in ListFlags (v2.3.0)\n\n**API:** REST and gRPC ListFlags endpoints\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nserver:\n  api:\n    list_flags:\n      include_metadata: false  # Default: only include when explicitly requested\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Control whether flag metadata is exposed in public ListFlags responses. Useful for hiding internal metadata in public APIs.\n\n**Example Request:**\n```\nGET /api/v1/namespaces/default/flags?include_metadata=true\n```\n\n### Git Fetch Policy Configuration (v2.3.0)\n\n**API:** Storage Backend Configuration\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nstorage:\n  default:\n    fetch_policy: \"lenient\"  # or \"strict\" (default)\n```\n\n**Options:**\n- `strict` (default): Fail startup if remote fetch fails\n- `lenient`: Continue with stale local data if fetch fails\n\n**Use Case:** Enable offline/degraded mode for resilience when Git remote is temporarily unavailable.\n\n### Custom Commit and PR Templates (v2.5.0)\n\n**API:** Storage Configuration\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\nstorage:\n  default:\n    templates:\n      commit_message: \"[flipt] {{.Resource}} {{.Action}}\\n\\nAutomated change by {{.Author}}\"\n      proposal_title: \"Flipt: Update features on {{.Base.Ref}}\"\n      proposal_body: \"Automated proposal\\n\\n- Branch: {{.Branch.Ref}}\\n- Base: {{.Base.Ref}}\"\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Customize commit messages and pull request titles/bodies for Git integration.\n\n### Boolean Evaluation Enhanced (v2.2.0)\n\n**API:** Evaluation Service\n**New Response Field:** Boolean evaluation responses now include matched `segment_keys`\n\n**Example:**\n```json\n{\n  \"enabled\": true,\n  \"reason\": \"MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON\",\n  \"segment_keys\": [\"segment_1\", \"segment_2\"],\n  \"flag_key\": \"my_flag\",\n  \"timestamp\": \"2026-02-17T00:00:00Z\"\n}\n```\n\n**Benefit:** Provides visibility into which segments matched, improving debuggability.\n\n### Streaming Evaluation (v2.1.1+)\n\n**API:** ClientEvaluationService\n**Endpoint:**\n```\nGET /client/v2/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{key}/stream\n```\n\n**Message Types:**\n```protobuf\nrpc EvaluationSnapshotNamespaceStream(EvaluationNamespaceSnapshotStreamRequest)\n  returns (stream EvaluationNamespaceSnapshot)\n```\n\n**Use Case:** Real-time evaluation data streaming for dashboard updates or client-side caching.\n\n---\n\n## Deprecations\n\n### v1 API Endpoints (Deprecated since v2.0.0)\n\n**Endpoints Affected:**\n- `GET /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags`\n- `GET /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags/{flag_key}`\n- `POST /api/v1/namespaces/{namespace_key}/flags`\n- And other `/api/v1/*` endpoints\n\n**Replacement:** Use v2 Environments and Resources APIs:\n```\nGET /api/v2/environments/{environment_key}/namespaces/{namespace_key}/resources/flipt.core.Flag\n```\n\n**Timeline:** Removal planned ~1 year after v2.0.0 (August 2025 → August 2026)\n\n### Database-Backed Storage (Deprecated since v2.0.0)\n\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\n# DEPRECATED - do not use\nstorage:\n  type: postgres\n  connection_string: \"...\"\n```\n\n**Replacement:** Git-based storage is now the standard\n```yaml\n# Use this instead\nstorage:\n  default:\n    remote: \"...\"\n    backend:\n      type: \"memory\"\n```\n\n**Timeline:** Complete removal planned ~1 year after v2.0.0\n\n### Session Token Offset Parameter (Deprecated in v2.0+)\n\n**API:** ListFlagRequest\n**Deprecated Field:**\n```protobuf\nmessage ListFlagRequest {\n  int32 limit = 1;\n  int32 offset = 2 [deprecated = true];  // DEPRECATED: use page_token instead\n  string page_token = 3;  // USE THIS\n  ...\n}\n```\n\n**Migration:**\n```go\n// Before\nGetFlags(ctx, &flipt.ListFlagRequest{\n  Offset: 0,\n  Limit: 10,\n})\n\n// After\nGetFlags(ctx, &flipt.ListFlagRequest{\n  Limit: 10,\n  PageToken: \"\", // empty string for first page\n})\n```\n\n### OpenTelemetry gRPC Interceptors (Deprecated in v2.4.0)\n\n**Configuration:**\n```yaml\n# DEPRECATED gRPC interceptor style\nserver:\n  grpc:\n    interceptors:\n      - otel\n```\n\n**Replacement:** Stats handler approach (automatically used in v2.4.0+)\n```go\n// Modern approach - automatically configured\n// Use stats handler for complete tracing coverage\n```\n\n---\n\n## Bug Fixes\n\n### Critical Fixes\n\n#### Segment Deletion Validation (v2.3.0)\n- **Issue:** Segments could be deleted even when referenced by rules or rollouts\n- **Fix:** Added validation to prevent deletion of segments with active references\n- **Impact:** Database integrity protection\n\n#### Streaming Evaluation HTTP/2 Support (v2.1.1)\n- **Issue:** Streaming evaluation only worked over gRPC, not HTTP/2\n- **Fix:** Accept HTTP/2 connections for streaming evaluation endpoints\n- **Impact:** Broader client compatibility\n\n#### Environment Context Handling (v2.1.1)\n- **Issue:** Error not returned when environment not found in context\n- **Fix:** Properly return error from `GetFromContext` when environment missing\n- **Impact:** Better error diagnostics\n\n### Important Fixes\n\n#### Git Remote Synchronization (v2.4.0)\n- **Issue:** Git remote URLs could become stale if configuration changed\n- **Fix:** Auto-sync remote URLs when configuration is updated\n- **Impact:** Prevents sync failures after config changes\n\n#### Git Connection Error Handling (v2.4.0)\n- **Issue:** DNS resolution and I/O timeout errors not properly detected\n- **Fix:** Properly handle connection errors to allow lenient fetch policy to work\n- **Impact:** Better offline mode support\n\n#### Git Ref Update After Fetch (v2.6.0)\n- **Issue:** Shallow fetches didn't persist references to `refs/heads/`\n- **Fix:** Complete ref update for both bare and normal repositories\n- **Impact:** Proper branch tracking in Git operations\n\n#### Evaluation Environment Lookup (v2.3.1)\n- **Issue:** Duplicate environment retrieval logic with inconsistent fallback behavior\n- **Fix:** Extracted reusable helper method for consistent lookup\n- **Impact:** Reduced code duplication and bugs\n\n#### License Key Format Handling (v2.6.0)\n- **Issue:** License activation wizard failed with annual license key format\n- **Fix:** Support both monthly and annual license key formats\n- **Impact:** Improved Pro license activation UX\n\n#### Redis Configuration Schema (v2.3.0)\n- **Issue:** Redis mode configuration accepted empty string\n- **Fix:** Set default Redis mode to \"single\" instead of empty string\n- **Impact:** More explicit configuration validation\n\n#### Form Submission UX (v2.3.0, v2.3.1)\n- **Issue:** Enter key in tag input submitted entire form unexpectedly\n- **Fix:** Properly handle Enter key for tag addition vs form submission\n- **Impact:** Better UI form handling\n\n### Analytics Fixes\n\n#### Batch Analytics Processing (v2.1.3)\n- **Issue:** Batch analytics returned partial data for requested flags\n- **Fix:** Return all batch data for requested flags\n- **Impact:** Complete analytics visibility\n\n#### Analytics Metadata Events (v2.3.0)\n- **Issue:** Batch evaluation endpoint missing OpenTelemetry tracking\n- **Fix:** Add event tracking to batch evaluation for observability\n- **Impact:** Complete tracing of batch operations\n\n---\n\n## Upgrade Guide\n\n### General Upgrade Process\n\n1. **Backup Configuration and Data**\n   ```bash\n   # Export current flags if upgrading from v1\n   flipt export --format=json > flags_backup.json\n\n   # Backup Git repository\n   git clone --mirror https://your-repo.git flipt-backup.git.mirror\n   ```\n\n2. **Update Flipt Binary**\n   ```bash\n   # Using official releases\n   curl -fsSL https://install.flipt.io | bash\n\n   # Or using container\n   docker pull flipt/flipt:v2.7.0\n   ```\n\n3. **Update Configuration File**\n   Review `/etc/flipt/config.yml` (or your config location) for required changes:\n   - Storage configuration format\n   - Authentication method configurations\n   - Any deprecated options\n\n4. **Verify Environment Variables**\n   Check all `FLIPT_*` environment variables are compatible with new version\n\n5. **Start Flipt**\n   ```bash\n   systemctl restart flipt\n   # or\n   docker-compose up -d\n   ```\n\n6. **Verify Endpoints**\n   Test core endpoints:\n   ```bash\n   # Health check\n   curl http://localhost:8080/health\n\n   # Verify APIs\n   curl -H \"Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN\" http://localhost:8080/api/v2/environments\n   ```\n\n### Upgrading from v1.x to v2.0+\n\nThis is a major upgrade requiring data migration:\n\n**Step 1: Set Up Git Repository**\n```bash\n# Create new Git repository for flags\nmkdir flipt-features && cd flipt-features\ngit init\ngit branch -m main\n\n# Or use existing repository\ngit clone https://github.com/your-org/flipt-features.git\ncd flipt-features\n```\n\n**Step 2: Update Configuration**\n```yaml\n# New v2 configuration\nstorage:\n  default:\n    remote: \"https://github.com/your-org/flipt-features.git\"\n    branch: \"main\"\n    backend:\n      type: \"local\"\n      path: \"/var/lib/flipt/storage\"\n    credentials:\n      type: \"github\"\n      token: \"${secret:file:/etc/flipt/github_token}\"\n\nenvironments:\n  default:\n    storage: \"default\"\n```\n\n**Step 3: Migrate Flags (if applicable)**\n```bash\n# Option A: Export from v1, import to v2\nflipt export --format=json > /tmp/flags.json\nflipt import --format=json < /tmp/flags.json\n\n# Option B: Manually recreate flags via v2 API\n# See Section \"New Features\" for v2 API examples\n```\n\n**Step 4: Update Client SDKs**\n```go\n// Before (v1)\nclient := flipt.NewClient(\n    flipt.WithAddress(\"localhost:8080\"),\n)\nresult, _ := client.Evaluation.Boolean(ctx, &flipt.EvaluationRequest{\n    NamespaceKey: \"default\",\n    FlagKey: \"my_flag\",\n    EntityId: \"user-123\",\n    Context: map[string]string{},\n})\n\n// After (v2)\nclient := flipt.NewClient(\n    flipt.WithAddress(\"localhost:8080\"),\n)\nresult, _ := client.Evaluation.Boolean(ctx, &flipt.EvaluationRequest{\n    EnvironmentKey: \"production\",  // NEW: required\n    NamespaceKey: \"default\",\n    FlagKey: \"my_flag\",\n    EntityId: \"user-123\",\n    Context: map[string]string{},\n})\n```\n\n### Upgrading from v2.x to v2.y\n\nThese are minor/patch upgrades with good backwards compatibility:\n\n**For v2.5.0 → v2.6.0:**\n- GitHub App auth is now available as an alternative to PAT\n- OIDC configuration enhancements are optional\n- No breaking changes; upgrade and redeploy\n\n**For v2.6.0 → v2.7.0:**\n- Go 1.26 and go-git v6 dependencies updated\n- SigV4 auth for Prometheus is optional\n- Default Git commit email updated (more appropriate value)\n- No breaking changes; standard upgrade\n\n**For any v2.x upgrade:**\n```bash\n# 1. Pull latest version\ndocker pull flipt/flipt:v2.7.0\n\n# 2. Verify configuration compatibility\nflipt validate-config --config=/etc/flipt/config.yml\n\n# 3. Restart Flipt\nsystemctl restart flipt\n# or\ndocker-compose up -d --force-recreate\n\n# 4. Check logs for warnings/errors\njournalctl -u flipt -f\n# or\ndocker-compose logs -f flipt\n```\n\n### Configuration Validation\n\nFlipt provides a validation command:\n```bash\n# Validate configuration before deploying\nflipt validate-config --config=./config.yml\n\n# With verbose output\nflipt validate-config --config=./config.yml --verbose\n```\n\n### Monitoring the Upgrade\n\nKey metrics to monitor:\n\n1. **API Error Rate**\n   ```promql\n   rate(flipt_server_request_duration_count{status=\"error\"}[5m])\n   ```\n\n2. **Evaluation Latency**\n   ```promql\n   histogram_quantile(0.95,\n     rate(flipt_evaluation_duration_seconds_bucket[5m])\n   )\n   ```\n\n3. **Git Sync Status**\n   ```promql\n   flipt_git_sync_duration_seconds_bucket\n   flipt_git_sync_errors_total\n   ```\n\n### Rollback Procedures\n\n**For Docker Deployments:**\n```bash\n# Quickly rollback to previous version\ndocker-compose down\ndocker-compose.yml  # Restore previous version\ndocker-compose up -d\n```\n\n**For Systemd Deployments:**\n```bash\nsystemctl stop flipt\n# Restore previous binary version\n/usr/local/bin/flipt  # downgrade\nsystemctl start flipt\n```\n\n**For Kubernetes Deployments:**\n```bash\n# Rollback to previous deployment\nkubectl rollout undo deployment/flipt\nkubectl rollout status deployment/flipt\n```\n\n### Common Troubleshooting\n\n**Issue: 404 errors on v1 API endpoints**\n- **Cause:** v1 endpoints are still available but require explicit migration\n- **Solution:** Update client SDKs to use v2 APIs as documented in \"Breaking Changes\"\n\n**Issue: Git authentication failures after upgrade**\n- **Cause:** Credentials format may have changed\n- **Solution:** Verify Git credentials in configuration using secret references\n\n**Issue: Evaluation requests return \"environment not found\"**\n- **Cause:** `environment_key` parameter now required (v2.3.0+)\n- **Solution:** Update evaluation requests to include explicit `environment_key` field\n\n**Issue: High memory usage after upgrade**\n- **Cause:** OpenTelemetry or ClickHouse integration in v2.1.3+ requires tuning\n- **Solution:** Review analytics storage configuration and batch buffer settings\n\n---\n\n## Summary Table\n\n| Feature | Introduced | Status | Migration Required |\n|---------|------------|--------|-------------------|\n|
